Historical Mysteries14 September 2026
Historical Mysteries › Sources left out

Working files

Sources left out of the public repository

The working folders behind the cases held copies of published sources that the research downloaded: scans, OCR text, web pages, articles, search excerpts. Copies of works still in copyright are not republished here or in the repository. This page lists each such file (2633 across 15 cases), what it was and where it came from, so that anyone re-running the code can fetch the same material. Copies of works in the public domain, and of material under an open licence, stay in the working files under their own terms.
